Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a ecology algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 167. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 85/4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on ecology structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 167. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 85. QED.

Theorem 219.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For any ecology module M satisfying the optimality condition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 944. The projective resolution has length at most 595 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: By the optimality condition principle applied to ecology theory, we decompose the problem into 944 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 595. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
